data-next-head="">{"@context":"https://schema.org","@type":"Person","affiliation":{"@type":"SportsTeam","logo":"https://drop-assets.ea.com/images/5VXhqALaBzgvb2YG8iJxlq/990024f3e3ecc9e6c37eae41ebef86bd/l241.png","name":"FC Barcelona","sport":"Association Football"},"image":"https://ratings-images-prod.pulse.ea.com/FC25/full/player-portraits/p277643.png?padding=0.7","jobTitle":{"@type":"OrganizationRole","namedPosition":"Right Wing"},"name":"Lamine Yamal","url":"https://www.ea.com/games/ea-sports-fc/ratings/player-ratings/lamine-yamal/277643"}</script><script id="named-capture-groups-polyfill" data-nscript="beforeInteractive">(function () {
    // On modern browsers this returns silently and the polyfill is never installed.
    try {
        if (new RegExp('(?<t>t)').exec('t').groups.t === 't') return;
    } catch (e) {}

    var NativeRegExp = RegExp;

    /**
     * Walk a raw regex source string and:
     *  - Replace (?<name>...) with (...)
     *  - Replace \k<name> with \n (positional backreference)
     *  - Track a capture-index-to-name map
     *
     * Non-capturing groups (?:...), lookaheads (?=...) (?!...), and
     * lookbehinds (?<=...) (?<!...) are passed through unchanged and do
     * NOT increment the capture index.
     */
    function parsePattern(src) {
        var nameMap = [null]; // 1-indexed; nameMap[captureIndex] = name | null
        var nameToIndex = {};
        var i = 0;
        var out = '';

        while (i < src.length) {
            var ch = src[i];

            if (ch === '\\') {
                // Named backreference: \k<name> → \captureIndex
                if (src.slice(i, i + 3) === '\\k<') {
                    var backrefEnd = src.indexOf('>', i + 3);
                    if (backrefEnd !== -1) {
                        var refName = src.slice(i + 3, backrefEnd);
                        var refIdx = nameToIndex[refName];
                        out += refIdx !== undefined ? '\\' + refIdx : src.slice(i, backrefEnd + 1);
                        i = backrefEnd + 1;
                        continue;
                    }
                }
                // Any other escape sequence — pass through as-is
                out += ch + (src[i + 1] || '');
                i += 2;
                continue;
            }

            if (ch === '(') {
                // Named capture group: (?<name>...) where next chars are NOT (?<=) or (?<!)
                if (src[i + 1] === '?' && src[i + 2] === '<' &&
                    src[i + 3] !== '=' && src[i + 3] !== '!' && src[i + 3] !== undefined) {
                    var nameEnd = src.indexOf('>', i + 3);
                    if (nameEnd !== -1) {
                        var name = src.slice(i + 3, nameEnd);
                        nameMap.push(name);
                        nameToIndex[name] = nameMap.length - 1;
                        out += '('; // Replace (?<name> with (
                        i = nameEnd + 1;
                        continue;
                    }
                }
                // Non-capturing / lookahead / lookbehind — starts with (?
                // Pass ( through; next iterations emit ?, :, =, !, < etc.
                if (src[i + 1] === '?') {
                    out += ch;
                    i++;
                    continue;
                }
                // Regular (unnamed) capture group — track but do not name
                nameMap.push(null);
                out += ch;
                i++;
                continue;
            }

            out += ch;
            i++;
        }

        return { source: out, nameMap: nameMap };
    }

    function PolyfillRegExp(pattern, flags) {
        if (typeof pattern !== 'string' || pattern.indexOf('(?<') === -1) {
            return new NativeRegExp(pattern, flags);
        }

        var parsed = parsePattern(pattern);
        var re = new NativeRegExp(parsed.source, flags);
        var nameMap = parsed.nameMap;

        var hasNames = false;
        for (var i = 0; i < nameMap.length; i++) {
            if (nameMap[i] !== null) { hasNames = true; break; }
        }
        if (!hasNames) return re;

        var origExec = re.exec.bind(re);
        re.exec = function (str) {
            var m = origExec(str);
            if (m) {
                var groups = Object.create(null);
                for (var j = 1; j < nameMap.length; j++) {
                    if (nameMap[j] !== null) groups[nameMap[j]] = m[j];
                }
                Object.defineProperty(m, 'groups', {
                    configurable: true,
                    value: groups,
                    writable: true
                });
            }
            return m;
        };

        return re;
    }

    // Assign to all possible global object references.
    // On most browsers window === self, but on some embedded WebViews
    // (e.g. Nintendo Switch) they may be distinct objects. The Next.js /
    // webpack runtime uses self as its global reference so both must
    // be covered; otherwise new RegExp() inside module code continues
    // to use the native engine constructor and the SyntaxError persists.
    try { window.RegExp = PolyfillRegExp; } catch (e) {}
    try { if (typeof self !== 'undefined') self.RegExp = PolyfillRegExp; } catch (e) {}

    // Keep the prototype chain intact so instanceof RegExp checks continue to work
    PolyfillRegExp.prototype = NativeRegExp.prototype;
    try { Object.setPrototypeOf(PolyfillRegExp, NativeRegExp); } catch (e) {}
})();</script><script id="browser-env-vars"
